~~~
# SOURCE
~~~sysml
package StateExports {
    private import ScalarValues::*;
    private import SI::*;
    port def Signal { in attribute enabled : Boolean; }
    state def Controller {
        port input : Signal;
        ref port proxy : Signal;
        attribute enabled : Boolean;
        attribute instant : Time::TimeInstantValue;
        action activate;
        action run;
        action deactivate;
        entry; then idle;
        state idle { entry activate; do run; exit deactivate; }
        state running {
            port nested : Signal;
            entry action enter { assign enabled := true; }
            do action execute { assign enabled := false; }
            exit action leave { assign enabled := false; }
        }
        transition timer first idle accept after 2 [s] then running;
        transition change first running accept when enabled and not false then idle;
        transition absolute first idle accept at instant then running;
    }
}
~~~
